## 二、数据集描述（平台原文）

> ⚠️ 平台目录描述存在**错配**（已实测：insiders1 / imbalance5 的描述指向无关数据集）。以「五、全字段明细」的真实字段语义为准。

This dataset provides a comprehensive mapping between Refinitiv Instrument Codes (RICs) and Bloomberg Identifiers (BBIDs), enabling seamless cross-referencing of securities across two major financial data platforms. By linking these identifiers, the dataset facilitates integration of market data, analytics, and research from both Bloomberg and Refinitiv sources. This mapping is essential for portfolio managers, analysts, and data engineers who need to reconcile, aggregate, or compare security-level information across systems. While not directly predictive of price movements, accurate identifier mapping is foundational for multi-source data analysis, backtesting, and the construction of robust trading signals and risk models.
